          <div style="text-align: center; line-height: 1.25; font-weight: bold;">PROPOSAL 5</div>
          <div style="text-align: center; line-height: 1.25; font-weight: bold;">RATIFICATION AND APPROVAL OF APPOINTMENT OF INDEPENDENT REGISTERED </div>
          <div style="text-align: center; line-height: 1.25; font-weight: bold;">PUBLIC ACCOUNTANTS</div>
          <div style="text-align: center; line-height: 1.25;">(Item 5 on the Proxy Card)</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; text-indent: 36pt; line-height: 1.25;">At the Meeting, shareholders will be asked to ratify and approve the re-appointment of Kost Forer Gabbay &amp; Kasierer, registered public accounting firm, a member of Ernst
            &amp; Young Global, as our independent registered public accountants for the fiscal year ending December 31, 2019 and for such additional period until our next annual general meeting of shareholders, pursuant to the recommendation of our Audit
            Committee and Board of Directors.&#160; Kost Forer Gabbay &amp; Kasierer has no relationship with us or any of our subsidiaries except as independent registered public accountants and, from time to time and to a limited extent, as tax consultants
            and providers of some audit-related and other services.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; text-indent: 36pt; line-height: 1.25;">In accordance with our Articles of Association and as permitted by the Israeli Companies Law, our Board of Directors is authorized to determine the compensation of our
            independent registered public accountants.&#160; Our Board of Directors determines such compensation following the pre-approval and recommendation of our Audit Committee.&#160; For details regarding the fees billed to us by Kost Forer Gabbay &amp;
            Kasierer for professional services rendered in 2018, see &#8220;Item 16C. Principal Accountant Fees and Services&#8221; of our annual report on Form 20-F for the year ended December 31, 2018, filed with the SEC on February 27, 2019.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; text-indent: 36pt; line-height: 1.25;">It is therefore proposed that at the Meeting the following resolution be adopted:</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; margin-right: 53.85pt; margin-left: 36pt; line-height: 1.25;">&#8220;RESOLVED, that the appointment of Kost Forer Gabbay &amp; Kasierer, a member of Ernst &amp; Young Global, as the independent registered public
              accountants of the Company for the year ending December 31, 2019 and for such additional period until the next annual general meeting of shareholders, be and hereby is ratified and approved.&#8221;</div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>

            and voting on the matter, is required to approve the foregoing resolution.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>

            statements for the year ended December 31, 2018.&#160; This Item will not involve a shareholder a vote.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; text-indent: 36pt; line-height: 1.25;">Our audited consolidated financial statements for the year ended December 31, 2018, which form part of our annual report on Form 20-F for the year ended December 31, 2018,
            filed with the SEC on February 27, 2019, are available on our website at <u>www.kamada.com</u> or through the EDGAR website of the SEC at <u>www.sec.gov</u> or through the Magna website of the Israel Securities Authority at <u>www.magna</u><u>.isa.gov.il</u>.
            Shareholders may receive a hard copy of the annual report on Form 20-F containing the consolidated financial statements free of charge upon request.&#160; None of the audited consolidated financial statements, the Form 20-F nor the contents of our
            website form part of the proxy solicitation material.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: center; line-height: 1.25; font-weight: bold;">SHAREHOLDER PROPOSALS</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; text-indent: 36pt; line-height: 1.25;">Any shareholder who intends to present a proposal at the Meeting must satisfy the requirements of the Israeli Companies Law and the regulations promulgated thereunder.&#160; Under
            Section 66(b) of the Israeli Companies Law, one or more shareholders who severally or jointly hold at least 1% of or outstanding voting rights are entitled to request that our Board of Directors include a proposal at a future shareholder
            meeting, provided that such proposal is appropriate for consideration by shareholders at such meeting, by submitting such proposal within seven days of publication of a company&#8217;s notice with respect to its general meeting of shareholders.&#160;
            Accordingly, any such shareholder(s) may request to include a proposal on the agenda of the Meeting by submitting their proposals in writing to Ms. Orna Naveh, our General Counsel and Corporate Secretary, at the following address: 2 Holzman
            Street, Weizmann Science Park, Rehovot, Israel, Attention: General Counsel and Corporate Secretary.&#160; For a shareholder proposal to be considered for inclusion at the Meeting, our General Counsel must receive the written proposal, together with
            the accompanying documentation and information required to be submitted under Israeli law, no later than September 26, 2019. If our Board of Directors determines that a shareholder proposal is duly and timely received and is appropriate under
            applicable Israeli law for inclusion on the agenda on the Meeting, we will publish a revised agenda for the Meeting no later than October 3, 2019, by way of issuing a press release or submitting a Report on Form 6-K to the SEC.</div>

            the judgment and recommendation of the Board of Directors.</div>

                          provisions of the Applicable Law shall prevail over this Policy and the Board is empowered hereunder to interpret and enforce such prevailing provisions.&#160; Whenever the context may require, any pronoun shall include the
                          corresponding masculine, feminine and neuter forms.&#160; The words &#8220;include&#8221;, &#8220;includes&#8221; and &#8220;including&#8221; shall be deemed to be followed by the phrase &#8220;without limitation&#8221;.&#160; References to any law or regulation, rule or ordinance,
                          including any section or other part thereof, shall refer to that as amended from time to time and shall include any successor law.&#160; The use of captions and titles in this Policy is for the convenience of reference only and shall
                          not affect the meaning of any provision of this Plan.</div>
                      </td>

                        <div>Nothing in this Policy shall confer upon any person, including, any Executive, any rights, entitlements, benefits or remedies whatsoever, including any right or entitlement to any compensation, remuneration or benefits of any
                          kind or nature or to interfere with or limit in any way the right and authority of the Company or any its Affiliates to determine any compensation, remuneration or benefits or to terminate the service or employment of any
                          Executive.&#160; The Terms of Office and Engagement of an Executive shall only be as set in an agreement between such Executive and the Company or its Affiliates or in a written undertaking of the Company or its Affiliates or in a
                          resolution of the relevant organ of the Company or such Affiliate setting forth the Terms of Office and Engagement and their applicability to the relevant Executive, and, in each case, as prescribed by Applicable Law.&#160; No
                          representation or warranty is made by the Company in adopting this Policy, and no custom or practice shall be inferred from this Policy or the implementation thereof, which is specific and applied on a case-by-case basis.</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                          shall apply,<font style="font-style: italic;"> mutatis mutandis</font>, to the same extent as if the service or engagement would have been made pursuant to an agreement with the Executive personally.&#160; To the extent that an
                          Executive&#8217;s engagement or service is not through employment relations with the Company or any Affiliate thereof then this Policy shall apply,<font style="font-style: italic;"> mutatis mutandis</font>.</div>
                      </td>

                    </td>

              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">4.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div><font style="font-weight: bold; font-variant: small-caps;"><u>General Considerations</u></font>&#160;</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">4.1.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>This Policy is made, and the Terms of Office and Engagement determined pursuant hereto shall be determined, on the basis of various considerations, including those listed below.</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">4.1.1.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>The Compensation Policy was designed, among other things, to ensure the Company&#8217;s ability to recruit and retain the highly talented management personnel that have the appropriate qualifications needed by the Company, which is a
                          key element for the Company&#8217;s success.&#160; The Company believes that in order to attract and retain competent and skilled Executives that would support the efforts to create shareholder value, the Executives&#8217; Terms of Office and
                          Engagement should generally be comparable to Executives in comparable companies.&#160; In certain circumstances, in order to attract unique talents that are considered by the Company as such, the Terms of Office and Engagement may
                          exceed the above levels.</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">4.1.2.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>Promoting the Company&#8217;s objectives, its business plan and its long-term strategy.&#160; The Company believes that attracting and retaining Executives that have appropriate qualifications is one of the key elements to the Company's
                          success. In order to attract and retain Executives that possess skills, experience, professional capabilities and motivation that would support the Company&#8217;s efforts to increase shareholder value, the Terms of Office and
                          Engagement under which such Executives are retained should be competitive, should reflect the anticipated contribution of such Executives to the Company and its business, should reflect the scope of authority and responsibilities
                          of the Executive and should create adequate incentives for such Executives to dedicate their full attention, skills and efforts to the success and growth of the Company.&#160;</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
            <div id="DSPFPageBreakArea" style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;">
              <div id="DSPFPageNumberArea" style="text-align: center;"><font id="DSPFPageNumber" style="font-weight: normal; font-style: normal; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">A 1 - 3</font></div>
              <div id="DSPFPageBreak" style="page-break-after: always;">
                <hr noshade="noshade" style="border-width: 0px; clear: both; margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000;"></div>
            </div>
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">4.1.3.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>Creating appropriate incentives to the Company&#8217;s Executives, considering, among other factors, the Company&#8217;s risk management policy.&#160; In this respect, the Company will strive to create balanced compensation arrangements under
                          which an Executive will be motivated to contribute to the achievement of the Company&#8217;s targets by creating a link between performance and compensation.&#160; On the other hand, attention will be given to the need to allocate an
                          appropriate portion to compensation that is not based on performance with a view to maintaining caution as to the tolerance of risk management.&#160; In addition, the Company believes that the Terms of Office and Engagement should
                          reflect a balance between short-term and long-term achievements, between personal performance of an Executive and performance of the Company or specific divisions or regions of the Company, between past performance and future
                          performance and taking into account various other considerations that are appropriate in each individual case.&#160; Moreover, the Company believes that the Terms of Office and Engagement of each Executive are both a reflection of the
                          Company&#8217;s general policies and the individual circumstances relating to the retention of such Executive, and therefore, there may be variations between the Terms of Office and Engagement of different Executives.</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">4.1.4.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>The size of the Company and the nature of its operations.&#160; The Company operates in an advanced international environment and in markets that are dynamic and are continuously in flux, offering multiple and different challenges.&#160;
                          Accordingly, in connection with the determination of the Terms of Office and Engagement of each Executive, appropriate attention should be given to the particular circumstances and challenges of such Executive.</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">4.1.5.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>The Terms of Office and Engagement of an Executive should generally be determined after consideration is given to the terms offered to comparable Executives in comparable companies, to the extent such information is readily
                          available, with a view to the Company&#8217;s ability to offer competitive terms and retain competent and capable Executives.</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">4.2.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>The Terms of Office and Engagement of an Executive may include a combination of various components, such as: salary and auxiliary payments and benefits, annual bonuses, special bonuses, equity or equity-linked awards, expense
                          reimbursement, insurance, exculpation and indemnification, and compensation and benefits mandated by Applicable Law.&#160; In each instance, the appropriate components should be considered, and not necessarily all of the above
                          mentioned components need be included.</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">5.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div style="font-variant: small-caps; font-weight: bold;"><u>Specific Considerations in the determination of Terms of Office and Engagement</u></div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 21.3pt; line-height: 1.25;">With a view to achieving the general purpose and intent of the considerations as set forth in Section&#160;4, the Terms of Office and Engagement of an Executive shall be
              predominantly based on the following considerations:</div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">5.1.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>The education, qualification, skills, expertise, professional experience, accomplishments, references, reputation and achievements of the Executive;&#160;</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
            <div id="DSPFPageBreakArea" style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;">
              <div id="DSPFPageNumberArea" style="text-align: center;"><font id="DSPFPageNumber" style="font-weight: normal; font-style: normal; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">A 1 - 4</font></div>
              <div id="DSPFPageBreak" style="page-break-after: always;">
                <hr noshade="noshade" style="border-width: 0px; clear: both; margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000;"></div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">5.2.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>If applicable, the experience, references, reviews, achievements and sustained performance of the Executive overtime with the Company and its Affiliates;</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>

            as to which components are appropriate and their respective weight.&#160; Any deviation of up to 10% from the ratios and caps set forth in this policy shall not be deemed as a deviation from this Policy.</div>

                          Board, and for the other Executives, the CEO).&#160; The base salary will be determined personally for each Executive based on the considerations detailed in Section 5.&#160; The Executives&#8217; salary may be linked to any relevant index.</div>

              aggregate limit of liability pursuant to the D&amp;O Insurance (including Side &#8220;A&#8221; coverage) shall be not more than US$50,0000,000 (fifty million U.S. Dollars) for each D&amp;O Insurance period; (ii) the annual premium for each D&amp;O
              Insurance (including Side &#8220;A&#8221; coverage) shall not exceed US$750,000 (seven hundred and fifty thousand U.S. Dollars); (iii) the maximum aggregate deductible payable by the Company shall not exceed US$2,000,000 (two million U.S. Dollars); and
              (iv) the D&amp;O Insurance is on market terms and shall not have a material impact on the Company&#8217;s profitability, assets or liabilities. </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; text-indent: 39.6pt; line-height: 1.25;">Any of the above benefits may include gross up of taxes and mandatory payments required to be made by Applicable Law.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>

                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.1.3.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div><u>Termination Payments</u></div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; text-indent: 39.6pt; line-height: 1.25;">Executives&#8217; termination payments may include one or more of the following:</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 54pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">6.1.3.1.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div><font style="font-style: italic;"><u>Advance Notice</u></font>.&#160; Advance notice of termination, not exceeding the higher of (i) the period required by Applicable Law and (ii) up to four (4) months.&#160; During this period the
                        Executive will be entitled to payment of full compensation, including benefits, and may be requested to continue working at the discretion of the Company. The Company may waive an Executive&#8217;s services during the advance notice
                        period and pay the Executive in lieu thereof, including the value of benefits.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div id="DSPFPageBreakArea" style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;">
            <div id="DSPFPageNumberArea" style="text-align: center;"><font id="DSPFPageNumber" style="font-weight: normal; font-style: normal; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">A 1 - 8</font></div>
            <div id="DSPFPageBreak" style="page-break-after: always;">
              <hr noshade="noshade" style="border-width: 0px; clear: both; margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000;"></div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 54pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">6.1.3.2.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div style="font-style: italic;"><u>Termination and Retirement Payment</u></div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 86.4pt; line-height: 1.25;">The Terms of Office and Engagement of an Executive may include a termination or retirement payment (in addition to any mandatory severance payments under Applicable Law),
            as follows:</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 86.4pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">&#8226;</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>if, at the time of termination, the Executive has been employed by the Company for at least three (3) years &#8211; up to two (2) monthly (gross) base salaries.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 86.4pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">&#8226;</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>if, at the time of termination, the Executive has been employed by the Company for at least five (5) years - up to four (4) monthly (gross) base salaries.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 86.4pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">&#8226;</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>if, at the time of termination, the Executive has been employed by the Company for at least ten (10) years&#160; - up to six (6) monthly (gross) base salaries.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 86.45pt; line-height: 1.25;">When considering the award of a termination payment, the Compensation Committee and Board shall take into consideration the Executive&#8217;s term of employment, the Executive&#8217;s
            compensation during employment with the Company, the Company&#8217;s performance during such period, the contribution of the Executive to achieving the Company&#8217;s goals and the circumstances of termination.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.2.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div><u>Variable Compensation</u></div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.2.1.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify; font-weight: normal; color: rgb(0, 0, 0); font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif; font-style: normal; font-variant: normal; text-transform: none;">
                        <div>The Company believes that the Terms of Office and Engagement should reflect a balance between short-term and long-term achievements, between personal performance of an Executive and performance of the Company or specific
                          divisions of the Company, between past performance and future performance and taking into account various other considerations that are appropriate in each individual case.&#160; Therefore, the Company believes that the ratio between
                          an Executive&#8217;s &#8220;fixed compensation&#8221; (comprised of base salary and benefits) to &#8220;variable compensation&#8221; (including, without limitation, annual cash bonuses and the value of equity-based compensation granted during the calendar
                          year, but excluding one-time cash awards such as special bonuses and termination bonuses), with respect to any given calendar year, may be up to 1:2 for the CEO (i.e., the total variable compensation may be up to two times the
                          total fixed compensation paid to the CEO in any calendar year) and up to 1:1 for Executives other than the CEO (i.e., the total variable compensation may be up to one time the total fixed compensation paid to the executive in any
                          calendar year).</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>

          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 72pt; line-height: 1.25;">The CEO shall inform the Committee of any awards to Executives (other than the CEO) determined by the CEO in accordance with the authority granted under this Section 6.2.2 at
            the first Committee meeting to be held after the determination by the CEO of each such award.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 72pt; line-height: 1.25;">Subject to Applicable Law, the Committee and Board shall determine and approve if and to what extent the measureable criteria (determined in accordance with the authority
            granted under this Section 6.2.2) have been achieved with respect to the CEO and all other Executives.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">

                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div><u>Bonuses</u>.&#160; Bonuses may include plan-based annual bonuses and other bonuses:</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">6.2.3.1.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div><u>Annual Bonus</u>. Executives may be incentivized through an annual bonus program that sets performance targets based on their role and scope.</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                    <td style="width: 86.4pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 17.85pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(a)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>With respect to each year, a bonus plan (the &#8220;<font style="font-weight: bold;">Bonus Plan</font>&#8221;) may be prepared for each Executive, as provided in clause (b) below, subject to Sections 6.2.1 and 6.2.1.&#160; The Bonus Plan may, but
                        shall not be required to, be set out in individual agreements with the applicable Executives.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 86.4pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 17.85pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(b)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The Bonus Plan will be comprised primarily of a measurable component and a small portion may be evaluation based, as follows:</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 104.25pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(i)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div><font style="font-weight: bold; font-style: italic;"><u>Measurable Component</u></font>:&#160; A significant portion of the annual bonus, not less than 80%, shall be determined based on measureable criteria, as follows.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 122.25pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">&#8226;</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div><font style="font-weight: bold; font-style: italic;"><u>Company Performance Criteria</u></font><font style="font-weight: bold; font-style: italic;">.</font>&#160; Quantifiable and measurable Company performance criteria shall be
                        determined and shall be the same for all Executives.&#160; The extent of meeting these measures shall determine 80% of the total bonus for the CEO and 40% of the total bonus for other Executives.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 122.25pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">&#8226;</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div><font style="font-weight: bold; font-style: italic;"><u>Individual Performance Criteria</u></font><font style="font-weight: bold; font-style: italic;">.</font>&#160; Quantifiable and measurable key performance indicators (KPIs) shall
                        be determined for each Executive separately, in accordance with his/her position. The extent of meeting these measures shall determine 40% of the total bonus of an Executive other than the CEO.&#160; No personal measures shall be
                        determined for the CEO.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div id="DSPFPageBreakArea" style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;">
            <div id="DSPFPageNumberArea" style="text-align: center;"><font id="DSPFPageNumber" style="font-weight: normal; font-style: normal; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">A 1 - 10</font></div>
            <div id="DSPFPageBreak" style="page-break-after: always;">
              <hr noshade="noshade" style="border-width: 0px; clear: both; margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000;"></div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 126pt; line-height: 1.25;">Measurable criteria may include the following: financial measures (such as net revenues; sales turnover, operating profit, percentage of operating profit, EBITDA, net
            profit, operating profit; earnings per share (EPS) and cash flow); obtaining regulatory approvals from the authorities in the target markets; achievement of operational, organizational and/or managerial goals, achievement of clinical and/or
            R&amp;D milestones; quality objectives; success in raising capital; meeting the Company&#8217;s budget; business development goals; and mergers and acquisitions.&#160; Each such criteria may constitute up to 50% of each of the total Company and individual
            performance criteria.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 108pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(ii)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div><font style="font-weight: bold; font-style: italic;"><u>Discretionary Component (Managerial Appraisal)</u></font>:&#160; The Company may determine that a portion of an Executive&#8217;s annual cash bonus will be based on the evaluation of
                        an Executive&#8217;s performance (by the Board in the case of the CEO and by the CEO in the case of all other Executives) in terms that are not measurable but which have a contribution to the Company&#8217;s long-term performance.&#160; The
                        managerial appraisal will determine 20% of an Executive&#8217;s total annual bonus.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 72pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 17.85pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(c)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The following will be considered when determining the allocation of the above criteria, which may differ from one Executive to another: (i)&#160;emphasizing the high level of accountability to overall Company performance and financial
                        results expected of each Executive; (ii)&#160;creating a personal link between each Executive&#8217;s compensation and the achievement of the corporate goals; and (iii)&#160;driving individuals to a high-performance culture.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 72pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 17.85pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(d)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The criteria and the method of measuring the criteria underlying the bonuses may differ from period to period and from one Executive to another.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 72pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 17.85pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(e)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The payment of any annual bonus to any Executives under the Bonus Plan for a given year shall be subject to a minimum percentage of achievement of the Company performance criteria for such calendar year, less than which
                        percentage Executives shall not be paid any annual bonus whatsoever for such calendar year; the minimum percentage is 70%.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 72pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 17.85pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(f)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The maximum bonus amount per year under the Bonus Plan that an Executive will be entitled to receive for any given calendar year may not exceed fifteen (15) (gross) monthly salaries for the CEO and twelve (12) (gross) monthly
                        salaries for an Executive other than the CEO.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 72pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 17.85pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(g)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>To the extent applicable, the Bonus Plan may be revisited during the annual period, including in order to account for significant changes in the Company&#8217;s business or operations or material changes in the market(s) in which the
                        Company operates during such year.&#160; Without limiting the foregoing, the authorized body shall be entitled to reduce or cancel an Executive&#8217;s annual bonus at his or its discretion.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 72pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 17.85pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(h)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>An Executive whose employment shall commence during a bonus year will be entitled to a pro-rated bonus, provided that the Executive has been employed for at least four months during the bonus year; provided, however, that under
                        special circumstances, the authorized body may determine that an Executive whose employment was for less than four months during the bonus year shall be entitled to a pro-rated bonus.&#160; An Executive whose employment terminated during
                        a bonus year (including upon a change of control event) may be entitled to a pro-rated bonus for that year, other than if the employment was terminated for Cause (as such term shall be defined in the Executive&#8217;s individual agreement
                        and in the absence thereof, as defined in the Equity Plan), in which case an Executive shall not be entitled to an annual bonus.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>

            <div id="DSPFPageBreak" style="page-break-after: always;">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 54pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">6.2.3.2.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div><u>Special Bonuses</u></div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 72pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(a)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>An Executive may be awarded a special cash bonus under special circumstances (&#8220;<font style="font-weight: bold;">Special Bonuses</font>&#8221;). Special Bonuses shall be payable in recognition of the achievement by the Company (or the
                        applicable Affiliate or division) or the Executive of specific goals (which may be similar to performance criteria as described above under &#8220;Annual Bonus&#8221;) or the occurrence of specific events or in recognition of the Executive&#8217;s
                        special contribution to key Company developments and activities (such as, without limitation, execution of projects not within the scope of the annual work plan, special efforts to execute a project within the scope of the annual
                        work plan and special contribution to the Company&#8217;s success and promotion of its goals), as well as to enable the Company to adapt to specific or unaccounted for changes or events that occur during the year, or as a signing cash
                        bonus.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 72pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">(b)</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The Special Bonus payable to an Executive may be up to six (6) times the monthly gross base salary (in addition to any annual bonus (if any)).</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.3.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div><u>Equity-Based Compensation</u></div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.3.1.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>Equity-based awards may be granted to Executives under the Company&#8217;s 2011 Israeli Share Award Plan (as may be amended from time to time) and such other equity plans for employees of the Company or its Affiliates that the Company
                        may adopt from time to time (the &#8220;<font style="font-weight: bold;">Equity Plans</font>&#8221;).&#160; Equity-based awards may include (without limitation): options to purchase shares of the Company, share appreciation rights, restricted share
                        units, restricted share awards, performance based awards and any other type of equity compensation that is based on the Company&#8217;s securities and may be granted under applicable tax regimes.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.3.2.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The maximum value of all equity-based awards, in the aggregate, that may be granted to a particular Executive on an annual basis shall not exceed 15 times the monthly (gross) base salary of an Executive in the case of the CEO and
                        12 times the monthly (gross) base salary of an Executive in the case of all other Executives.&#160; The maximum value of an equity-based award shall be determined as of the date of grant, other than cash-settled equity-based awards,
                        which shall be determined as of the date of payment.&#160; The value of Executives&#8217; equity-based awards will be determined in accordance with generally accepted valuation and accounting principles, as they apply to the relevant type of
                        equity-based award.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div id="DSPFPageBreakArea" style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;">
            <div id="DSPFPageNumberArea" style="text-align: center;"><font id="DSPFPageNumber" style="font-weight: normal; font-style: normal; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">A 1 - 12</font></div>
            <div id="DSPFPageBreak" style="page-break-after: always;">
              <hr noshade="noshade" style="border-width: 0px; clear: both; margin: 4px 0px; width: 100%; height: 2px; color: #000000; background-color: #000000;"></div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.3.3.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div> Equity-based awards to Executives shall be subject to an overall vesting period or reverse-vesting, as applicable, of no less than four years, with a minimum period of one (1) year from the date of grant prior to the vesting of
                        the first tranche, and at least 25% of an award shall vest in or with respect to each of the years.&#160; The maximum term of any equity-based award (prior to its expiration) shall be ten (10) years from the date of grant.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;" class="DSPFListTable">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.3.4.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>With respect to an equity-based award that includes an exercise price &#8211; the exercise price shall be equal to the higher of (i) the average closing price of the Company&#8217;s ordinary shares on the Tel Aviv Stock Exchange during the
                        30 trading days prior to the date of grant; and (ii) the closing price of the Company&#8217;s ordinary shares on the Tel Aviv Stock Exchange on the date of grant;</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.3.5.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The Company may determine to accelerate the vesting or continue the vesting and/or the exercise eligibility of an Executive&#8217;s equity-based awards after termination of such Executive&#8217;s employment, service or engagement or upon a
                        change of control event, in accordance with the provisions of the Equity Plans.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.3.6.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The Board and/or the Committee may amend other terms of an Executive&#8217;s grant(s) to the extent provided in the applicable Equity Plan and subject to Applicable Law.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>

                    </td>

                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>Subject to Applicable Law, a non-material amendment to the Terms of Office and Engagement of an Executive who is subordinate to the CEO (as compared to those approved by the Committee) shall not require the approval of the
                        Committee, provided that such amendment was approved by the CEO and the amended engagement terms are consistent with this Policy.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 39.4pt; line-height: 1.25;">The CEO shall inform the Committee of any amendment to the Terms of Office and Engagement of any such Executive approved by the CEO in accordance with the authority granted
            under this Section 6.4 at the first Committee meeting to be held after the approval by the CEO of each such amendment.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">

                      <td style="width: 17.85pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">7.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div style="font-variant: small-caps; font-weight: bold;"><u>Recoupment</u></div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 18pt; line-height: 1.25;">The Terms of Office and Engagement of an Executive shall include provisions that require an Executive to repay to the Company amounts paid to such Officer Holder as part of
              the Terms of Office and Engagement, if they were paid on the basis of figures that later transpire to be incorrect and were restated in the Company&#8217;s financial statements.&#160; The Compensation Committee shall be entitled to determine the amounts
              and conditions of such repayment, which may include terms under which (i) repayment will be made either on a pre-tax basis or an after-tax basis, unless and to the extent the Executive was able to recoup tax payments made with respect to the
              amounts to be repaid, (ii) no repayment obligation shall arise after the lapse of a period of time of no less than three years from the date on which the original payment was made, (iii) the period of time of no more than 12 months over which
              the repayment payments to the Company shall be made and the ability to make the repayment in installments or (to the extent permitted under Applicable Law) as a set-off against cash compensation paid by the Company to the Executive during
              such period , (iv) no repayment obligation shall arise in the event that the reason or basis for the restatement was due to changes in the Applicable Law, including generally acceptable accounting principles or financial reporting standards;
              and (v) such other provisions as determined in each case, subject to Applicable Law.&#160; Nothing in this Section 7 shall derogate from or limit any other or similar provisions imposed on an Executive by Applicable Law, including, securities
              laws.</div>

          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 18pt; line-height: 1.25;">This Compensation Policy (the &#8220;<font style="font-weight: bold;">Policy</font>&#8221;) constitutes the Compensation Policy (as such term is defined in the Companies Law) of Kamada
            Ltd. (the &#8220;<font style="font-weight: bold;">Company</font>&#8221;) with respect to the determination of Terms of Office and Engagement of non-employee members of the Board of Directors of the Company (each, a &#8220;<font style="font-weight: bold;">Director</font>&#8221;),


            if and to the extent such determination is required by the Companies Law be made pursuant to the Compensation Policy.</div>

              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="ze4e29b50260d40a68ad59ab1c6623a65" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">2.</td>

                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>

              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z0cdd441d6b1b4d78b5c7f5bf8dda9f74" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">2.1.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>&#8220;<font style="font-weight: bold;">Affiliate</font>&#8221; of any Person, shall mean any other Person that, directly or indirectly through one or more intermediaries, is controlled by such Person, and the term &#8220;control&#8221; (including the
                        terms &#8220;controlled by&#8221; and &#8220;under common control with&#8221;) means the possession, directly or indirectly, of the power to direct or cause the direction of the management and policies of such Person, whether through ownership of voting
                        securities, by contract or otherwise.&#160;</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

                          adjudicative authority or agency, of any jurisdiction, and the rules and regulations of any stock exchange or trading or quotation system on which the securities of the Company are then traded, listed or quoted.&#160;</div>

                      </td>

                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="ze1ddb5a55fe34ab3b102cb4d0869c3a9" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">4.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">

                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
