            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 18pt; line-height: 1.25;">This Policy is made, and the Terms of Office and Engagement determined pursuant hereto shall be determined, on the basis of various considerations, including those listed
              below.</div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="ze72fac6f5f8c4c8c9eb98dee33bdf157" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">4.1.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>The Compensation Policy was designed, among other things, to ensure the Company&#8217;s ability to attract and retain highly skilled Directors.&#160; In order to attract and retain Directors that possess skills, experience and
                          professional capabilities, the Terms of Office and Engagement under which such Directors are retained should be competitive.</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z1ac2196091a5479783c57b4ba31b38b8" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">4.2.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>The Terms of Office and Engagement of a Director should generally be determined after consideration is given to the terms offered to Directors in comparable companies, to the extent such information is readily available, with a
                          view to the Company&#8217;s ability to offer competitive terms and retain competent and capable Directors.</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="zd4a1438444d0469b9fa86f163883cf22" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">5.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div style="font-variant: small-caps; font-weight: bold;"><u>Specific Considerations in the determination of Terms of Office and Engagement</u></div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 18pt; line-height: 1.25;">With a view to achieving the general purpose and intent of the considerations as set forth in Section&#160;4, the Terms of Office and Engagement of a Director shall be
              predominantly based on the following considerations:</div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z3ab063f6c8834c12a95431977772dccf" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                      </td>
                      <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">5.1.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div>The education, qualification, skills, expertise, professional experience, accomplishments reputation and achievements of the Director;&#160;</div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z38efda74568f4c87a48b1ab37d4a9442" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">5.2.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The seniority, tenure and duration of the Director&#8217;s service to the Company or its Affiliates;</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="zb1d9147ccd7f4f8198407d38cd77f46e" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">5.3.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The obligations, responsibilities, roles and objectives imposed on such Director under Applicable Law;</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z3566c80304d24d909870697509c32ce9" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">5.4.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The need to retain Directors who have relevant skills, know-how or unique expertise; and</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z37125fecd59e45bcabbc22e4dceefac1" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">5.5.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>Prior Terms of Office and Engagement with the Company and its Affiliates.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z75428be0f204460b99acf73385800d07" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">5.6.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The compensation practices of comparable companies.&#160; The extent to which reference to comparable companies shall be required, as well as the parameters for determination of the identity of the companies which are comparable,
                        shall be examined in each instance.&#160; Such parameters may include: the field of operation or industry, public or privately held companies, size, local or global operations, business condition, numbers of years of operations and
                        jurisdiction of incorporation or of the executive headquarters;</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>

                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>If the Terms of Office and Engagement include variable components, inclusion of provisions reducing variable components, and setting a limit on the exercise value of an equity-based variable component, all at the Board&#8217;s
                        discretion;</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>

          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>

                    </td>
                  </tr>

                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">5.10.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>Such other considerations as are deemed relevant or applicable in the circumstances.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 18pt; line-height: 1.25;">The determination shall be made on the basis of all or part of the foregoing considerations and the weight of any particular consideration shall be as determined in the
              particular instance and based on the specific circumstances.</div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
            <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 18pt; line-height: 1.25;">The Company may, but shall not be required to, obtain advice from advisors and professionals for the purpose of assessing and determining the above considerations as the
              Company deems necessary, including, for the purpose of gathering relevant data, market research, labor practices and economic/cost analysis.</div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z4f9419d2ba7c4aa1904b85414bd5eb00" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">6.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div style="font-variant: small-caps; font-weight: bold;"><u>Components of Terms of Office and Engagement of a Director</u></div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 18pt; line-height: 1.25;">The Terms of Office and Engagement of a Director (including an External Director, within the meaning of the Companies Law, if serving on the Board) may include a combination
            of all or any part of the following components.&#160; In each instance, consideration shall be given as to which components are appropriate and their respective weight.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 18pt; line-height: 1.25;">The Terms of Office and Engagement of an External Director, within the meaning of the Companies Law, if serving on the Board, shall be subject to and determined in accordance
            with the Companies Law and regulations promulgated thereunder.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 18pt; line-height: 1.25;">Except as set forth in this Section 6, Directors shall not be entitled to any compensation, unless they are employed in an additional position at the Company, in which case
            their terms and conditions of employment shall be determined according to Company customary compensation and policies for similar positions, subject to the provisions of this Policy.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z283264fb19a94060a61e81a284aca663" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.1.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div><u>Annual Fee and Per Meeting Fees</u></div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 36pt; line-height: 1.25;">All Directors shall be paid an annual cash fee and per meeting fees in accordance with the Israeli Companies Regulations (Rules Regarding the Compensation and Expenses of an
            External Director), 5760-2000 (the &#8220;<font style="font-weight: bold;">Directors Compensation Regulations</font>&#8221;).</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 36pt; line-height: 1.25;">The Company may elect to pay increased fees to Directors who have accounting and financial expertise or certain professional expertise, within the meaning of and in
            accordance with the Director Compensation Regulations.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="clear: both; margin-top: 10pt; margin-bottom: 10pt;" id="DSPFPageBreakArea">

                      <div><u>Equity-Based Compensation</u></div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="zdd99fe4836e84621bb1f2d34e613a044" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.2.1.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>Equity-based awards may be granted to Directors under the Company&#8217;s 2011 Israeli Share Award Plan (as may be amended from time to time) and such other equity plans for employees and Directors of the Company or its Affiliates that
                        the Company may adopt from time to time (the &#8220;<font style="font-weight: bold;">Equity Plans</font>&#8221;).&#160; Equity-based awards may include (without limitation): options to purchase shares of the Company, share appreciation rights,
                        restricted share units, restricted share awards, performance based awards and any other type of equity compensation that is based on the Company&#8217;s securities and may be granted under applicable tax regimes.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="zbe20dc18c985401f80e9b93891fd64f3" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.2.2.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The maximum value of all equity-based awards, in the aggregate, that may be granted to a particular Director on an annual basis shall not exceed $50,000.&#160; The maximum value of an equity-based award shall be determined as of the
                        date of grant, other than cash-settled equity-based awards, which shall be determined as of the date of payment.&#160; The value of a Director&#8217;s equity-based awards will be determined in accordance with generally accepted valuation and
                        accounting principles, as they apply to the relevant type of equity-based award.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="zf9641ff784bb49349237c85631c78d43" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.2.3.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>Equity-based awards to Directors shall be subject to an overall vesting period or reverse-vesting, as applicable, of no less than four years, with a minimum period of one (1) year from the date of grant prior to the vesting of
                        the first tranche, and at least 25% of an award shall vest in or with respect to each of the years.&#160; The maximum term of any equity-based award (prior to its expiration) shall be ten (10) years from the date of grant.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z3df18035001c4516b98b319fe39ba2c4" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.2.4.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>With respect to an equity-based award that includes an exercise price &#8211; the exercise price shall be equal to the higher of (i) the average closing price of the Company&#8217;s ordinary shares on the Tel Aviv Stock Exchange during the
                        30 trading days prior to the date of grant; and (ii) the closing price of the Company&#8217;s ordinary shares on the Tel Aviv Stock Exchange on the date of grant;</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>

                  <tr>

                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The Company may determine to accelerate the vesting or continue the vesting and/or the exercise eligibility of a Director&#8217;s equity-based awards after termination of such Directors service or engagement or upon a change of control
                        event, in accordance with the provisions of the Equity Plans.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The Board and/or the Committee may amend other terms of a Director&#8217;s grant(s) to the extent provided in the applicable Equity Plan and subject to Applicable Law.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>

                      <div><u>Expenses</u></div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 36pt; line-height: 1.25;">All Directors may be reimbursed for their reasonable expenses incurred in connection with attending meetings of the Board and committee&#8217;s thereof (including domestic and
            international travel expenses) and travelling on behalf of the Company, consistent with the Company&#8217;s practices and policies.</div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>

              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z9ee1428efe6245feaa62d2b23a36871f" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>

                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.4.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div><u>Insurance, Exculpation and Indemnification</u></div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="z6552728847aa4612abfce96679c8d8a3" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.4.1.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The Company may indemnify, exculpate and insure Directors (including in their capacity as directors of the Company&#8217;s subsidiaries) to the fullest extent permitted by Applicable Law from time to time.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="text-align: justify; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div>
              <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="zb34dc75c4c884b60bcd6c8e5a2c51b57" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                  <tr>
                    <td style="width: 36pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 60pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">6.4.2.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>Subject to Applicable Law, the acquisition, extension, renewal or replacement of directors&#8217; and officers&#8217; liability insurance (&#8220;<font style="font-weight: bold;">D&amp;O Insurance</font>&#8221;) may be approved solely by the Committee
                        provided that (i) the maximum aggregate limit of liability pursuant to the D&amp;O Insurance (including Side &#8220;A&#8221; coverage) shall be not more than US$50,000,000 (fifty million U.S. Dollars) for each D&amp;O Insurance period; (ii) the
                        annual premium for each D&amp;O Insurance (including Side &#8220;A&#8221; coverage) shall not exceed US$750,000 (seven hundred and fifty thousand U.S. Dollars); (iii) the maximum aggregate deductible payable by the Company shall not exceed
                        US$2,000,000 (two million US Dollars); and (iv) the D&amp;O Insurance is on market terms and shall not have a material impact on the Company&#8217;s profitability, assets or liabilities. </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">

              <div>
                <table cellspacing="0" cellpadding="0" id="zd72a34d8426b4476b1308f9bfc597088" class="DSPFListTable" style="font-family: 'Times New Roman', Times, serif; font-size: 10pt; width: 100%; text-align: left; color: #000000;">

                    <tr>
                      <td style="width: 18pt; vertical-align: top; align: right;">7.</td>
                      <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                        <div style="font-variant: small-caps; font-weight: bold;"><u>Recoupment</u></div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>

                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>
          <div style="background-color: #FFFFFF; line-height: 1.25;">
            <div style="text-align: justify; margin-left: 18pt; line-height: 1.25;">The Terms of Office and Engagement of a Director shall include provisions that require a Director to repay to the Company amounts paid to such Directors as part of the
              Terms of Office and Engagement, if they were paid on the basis of figures that later transpire to be incorrect and were restated in the Company&#8217;s financial statements.&#160; The Compensation Committee shall be entitled to determine the amounts and
              conditions of such repayment, which may include terms under which (i) repayment will be made either on a pre-tax basis or an after-tax basis, unless and to the extent the Director was able to recoup tax payments made with respect to the
              amounts to be repaid, (ii) no repayment obligation shall arise after the lapse of a period of time of no less than three years from the date on which the original payment was made, (iii) the period of time of no more than 12 months over which
              the repayment payments to the Company shall be made and the ability to make the repayment in installments or (to the extent permitted under Applicable Law) as a set-off against cash compensation paid by the Company to the Director during such
              period , (iv) no repayment obligation shall arise in the event that the reason or basis for the restatement was due to changes in the Applicable Law, including generally acceptable accounting principles or financial reporting standards; and
              (v) such other provisions as determined in each case, subject to Applicable Law.&#160; Nothing in this Section 7 shall derogate from or limit any other or similar provisions imposed on a Director by Applicable Law, including, securities laws.</div>
            <div style="line-height: 1.25">&#160;</div>
          </div>

                    <td style="width: 18pt;"><br>
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 54pt; vertical-align: top; font-family: 'Times New Roman',Times,serif;">8.2.</td>
                    <td style="width: auto; vertical-align: top; text-align: justify;">
                      <div>The term of this Policy shall not be limited in time, except that it will terminate at the earlier of (i) such time that the Policy is no longer in effect under the Companies Law, or (ii) such time that the Policy is terminated
                        by the Board, to the extent that the Board has the power under the Companies Law to terminate the Policy, or (iii) such time that the determination of Terms of Office and Engagement of Directors is not required to be made pursuant
                        to a Compensation Policy under the Companies Law, including, without limitation, in the event that the Company ceases to be a Public Company (as defined in the Companies Law), in which case this Policy shall have no effect with
                        respect to Terms of Office and Engagement of Directors with respect to the period after the Company ceases to be a Public Company.</div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>

              </table>
            </div>

            any specific jurisdiction, which shall be governed by the respective laws of such jurisdiction.&#160; Certain definitions, which refer to laws other than the laws of such jurisdiction, shall be construed in accordance with such other laws.</div>
